#! Emerald Champions hand-authored campaign teams (see scripts/emerald_champions_teams.py)
#! EV units: HP/Atk/Def/SpA/SpD/Spe; each 0-252, total at most 510. Named spreads also use EVs.

# ---- E0001 Route103 cap=14 Opening rival battle
## E0001 TRAINER_BRENDAN_ROUTE_103_MUDKIP class=rival
strategy: SETUP
# Rival / double
plan: The unchosen regional starter leads beside Light Ball Pikachu: use Fake Out for a concrete setup or attacking window, or Electroweb when the speed change helps. The Hoenn alternatives are protected Swords Dance Treecko, direct physical Mudkip and Speed Boost special Torchic; other generations retain their actual native starter roles, with rival-only corrections for weather-dependent or redundant moves. Eviolite Shroomish supplies sleep and recovery pressure from reserve, while Toxic Orb Taillow is an independent Guts finisher. Preserve the rival's recurring partners without copying their role into every nearby trainer.
crack: Prepare around the actual unchosen starter, Pikachu speed control and the available reserves. Ground immunity, Fake Out denial, powder immunity, defensive positioning and calculated offensive pressure are distinct tools. The player can catch and prepare a broader party before this battle; neither of the two chosen starter families is imposed as a mandatory solution.

# ---- E0002 Route102 cap=14 Stone Badge
## E0002 TRAINER_CALVIN_1 class=regular
strategy: SETUP,TAILWIND
plan: Poochyena denies opposing support with Taunt or pressures with Crunch and Sucker Punch while bulky Zigzagoon looks for a survivable Belly Drum and immediate Sitrus recovery. Extreme Speed makes bulk more valuable than maximum Speed. Helping Hand is for an actual partner attack, not the setup turn. Tinted Lens Hoothoot provides special pressure and conditional Tailwind for surviving partners; its Speed investment lets it benefit from its own Wind on later turns, trading some bulk and damage for that opportunity. Reckless Starly retains immediate physical pressure and U-turn positioning instead of duplicating the rival's Toxic Orb Taillow.
crack: Double-target Zigzagoon or use Fake Out or Taunt before Belly Drum; redirect its single-target priority with Pachirisu. Ghosts ignore Extreme Speed and Hyper Voice but must respect the team's other attacks. Intimidate limits the physical members but not Hoothoot's special attacks. Rock and Electric pressure the bird reserves; Taunt denies Tailwind but not their damage. Tinted Lens weakens resistances, not immunities. Sitrus recovery is not Gluttony-dependent and the current engine permits its post-Drum trigger at either HP parity.

POOCHYENA @FOCUS_SASH RATTLED JOLLY 4/252/0/0/0/252 -2 | CRUNCH, TAUNT, SUCKER_PUNCH, HELPING_HAND | ivs=31/31/31/31/31/31 | friendship=255
ZIGZAGOON @SITRUS_BERRY GLUTTONY ADAMANT 252/252/4/0/0/0 -1 | BELLY_DRUM, EXTREME_SPEED, SEED_BOMB, PROTECT | ivs=31/31/31/31/31/31 | friendship=255
HOOTHOOT @EVIOLITE TINTED_LENS MODEST 164/0/8/204/0/132 0 | AIR_SLASH, HYPER_VOICE, TAILWIND, PROTECT | ivs=31/31/31/31/31/31 | friendship=255
STARLY @LIFE_ORB RECKLESS JOLLY 4/252/0/0/0/252 0 | BRAVE_BIRD, DOUBLE_EDGE, QUICK_ATTACK, U_TURN | ivs=31/31/31/31/31/31 | friendship=255

# ---- E0003 Route102 cap=14 Stone Badge
## E0003 TRAINER_RICK class=regular
strategy: NONE
plan: Choice Scarf lets Insomnia Spinarak act before Dwebble under the actual Gen 9 stats: Electroweb creates speed advantages while Toxic Thread can slow Electric absorbers without triggering their Electric absorption. Knock Off offers item disruption when control is poor; Choice lock and losing Focus Sash are real costs, and faster attackers can still deny the lead. Dwebble invests 188 Speed EVs to exploit a drop rather than remaining too slow behind its support. Sturdy and Eviolite cover different damage ranges, not a promise of invulnerability. No Guard Karrablast provides reliable Megahorn pressure from reserve; bulky special Water Bubble Dewpider changes the Fire matchup and attacks a different defense with Scald and Bug Buzz, with Giga Drain for coverage and recovery.
crack: Taunt denies Toxic Thread but not damaging Electroweb; Ground and Electric absorption block Electroweb but not Toxic Thread. Clear Body, priority and removing Spinarak attack the speed plan in different ways. Break Sturdy before committing to a knockout. Intimidate weakens the physical members but not Dewpider, whose Water attacks can still be absorbed or redirected. Fire is not a universal answer once Dewpider enters, and poison can benefit a Guts attacker. No Guard also makes attacks into Karrablast accurate.

SPINARAK @CHOICE_SCARF INSOMNIA JOLLY 252/0/4/0/0/252 -2 | ELECTROWEB, TOXIC_THREAD, POISON_JAB, KNOCK_OFF | ivs=31/31/31/31/31/31 | friendship=255
DWEBBLE @EVIOLITE STURDY ADAMANT 68/252/0/0/0/188 -1 | ROCK_SLIDE, X_SCISSOR, KNOCK_OFF, PROTECT | ivs=31/31/31/31/31/31 | friendship=255
KARRABLAST @LIFE_ORB NO_GUARD ADAMANT 4/252/0/0/0/252 0 | MEGAHORN, DRILL_RUN, KNOCK_OFF, PROTECT | ivs=31/31/31/31/31/31 | friendship=255
DEWPIDER @SITRUS_BERRY WATER_BUBBLE MODEST 252/0/4/252/0/0 0 | SCALD, BUG_BUZZ, GIGA_DRAIN, PROTECT | ivs=31/31/31/31/31/31 | friendship=255

# ---- E0004 Route102 cap=14 Stone Badge
## E0004 TRAINER_ALLEN class=regular
strategy: NONE
plan: Fast Light Ball Pikachu can Fake Out one foe while bulky Paras Spores the other. Paras can instead use priority Wide Guard to protect both partners from spread attacks while Pikachu commits to Volt Tackle; Leech Life supplies actual damage and recovery when support is unnecessary. Choice Band Slakoth is a reserve wallbreaker, not a claim that sleep removes Truant. Huge Power Bunnelby gives the second phase reliable Frustration at its native zero trainer friendship alongside priority, Rock Slide coverage and a pivot out of an unfavorable lock or matchup.
crack: A faster Fake Out, such as Scarf Mienfoo's, can disrupt Pikachu; Grass types and Safety Goggles prevent Spore. Taunt shuts off Spore and Wide Guard but not Leech Life. Wide Guard does not stop single-target attacks or Volt Tackle recoil; choosing protection should depend on the actual opposing options. Protect can waste Slakoth's attacking turn; bulky Ghosts deny Normal attacks but must respect Night Slash. Bunnelby is not Ground-type before evolution.

PIKACHU @LIGHT_BALL LIGHTNING_ROD JOLLY 4/252/0/0/0/252 0 | FAKE_OUT, VOLT_TACKLE, ELECTROWEB, PROTECT | ivs=31/31/31/31/31/31 | friendship=255
PARAS @EVIOLITE DRY_SKIN ADAMANT 252/252/4/0/0/0 -1 | SPORE, WIDE_GUARD, LEECH_LIFE, PROTECT | ivs=31/31/31/31/31/31 | friendship=255
SLAKOTH @CHOICE_BAND TRUANT ADAMANT 252/252/4/0/0/0 -1 | BODY_SLAM, HAMMER_ARM, NIGHT_SLASH, ROCK_SLIDE | ivs=31/31/31/31/31/31 | friendship=255
BUNNELBY @SITRUS_BERRY HUGE_POWER ADAMANT 4/252/0/0/0/252 0 | QUICK_ATTACK, FRUSTRATION, ROCK_SLIDE, U_TURN | ivs=31/31/31/31/31/31 | friendship=0

# ---- E0005 Route102 cap=14 Stone Badge
## E0005 TRAINER_TIANA class=casual
strategy: NONE
plan: Skitty uses Fake Out for an attacking window or Helping Hand for Swirlix's spread Dazzling Gleam; Sing is an inaccurate option, never the only route. Swirlix gains Unburden only after its Sitrus is consumed, retaining Draining Kiss for recovery and Thunderbolt for Water and Flying foes. Bulky Adaptability Eevee attacks physically and can set a delayed Wish for its field position. Klutz Buneary can pass Flame Orb to a burn-vulnerable physical foe, disrupt an exposed Protect or setup with Encore, or use Fake Out and Drain Punch when the item trade is bad. Orb burn begins at end turn, not before that turn's incoming attack.
crack: Deny the opening flinch or pressure Skitty. Steel and Poison resist Fairy; Wide Guard stops Gleam but not its single-target alternatives. Ground immunity and Electric redirection can deny Thunderbolt, and priority remains relevant after Unburden. Fire types, already-statused foes and Guts users can make Buneary's Flame Orb trade ineffective or actively harmful. Removing Swirlix before its berry activates denies the speed phase. Sing can miss and Soundproof blocks it; Grass or powder protection does not.

SKITTY @FOCUS_SASH WONDER_SKIN JOLLY 252/0/4/0/0/252 -2 | FAKE_OUT, SING, HELPING_HAND, PROTECT | ivs=31/31/31/31/31/31 | friendship=255
SWIRLIX @SITRUS_BERRY UNBURDEN MODEST 4/0/0/252/0/252 -2 | DRAINING_KISS, DAZZLING_GLEAM, THUNDERBOLT, PROTECT | ivs=31/31/31/31/31/31 | friendship=255
EEVEE @EVIOLITE ADAPTABILITY ADAMANT 252/252/4/0/0/0 -1 | QUICK_ATTACK, DOUBLE_EDGE, WISH, PROTECT | ivs=31/31/31/31/31/31 | friendship=255
BUNEARY @FLAME_ORB KLUTZ JOLLY 4/252/0/0/0/252 -1 | FAKE_OUT, SWITCHEROO, ENCORE, DRAIN_PUNCH | ivs=31/31/31/31/31/31 | friendship=255

# ---- E0006 Route104 cap=14 Stone Badge
## E0006 TRAINER_BILLY class=regular
strategy: RAIN
plan: Timid Lotad acts before bulky Modest Ducklett under the actual Gen 9 stats, so a successful Rain Dance can improve Hurricane on the setup turn. Lotad gains Swift Swim; Ducklett's Hydration supplies status recovery in rain. Surf can pressure both foes beside quarter-resistant Lotad but still damages its ally, and becomes riskier beside the reserves. Icy Wind is for useful speed crossings, not automatic extra setup. Do not reset useful rain. Scarf Imposter Ditto punishes an exposed boosted attacker, while Wide Lens Tandemaus threatens Population Bomb or uses Encore against a passive opponent.
crack: Fake Out or Taunt on Lotad can deny the rain setup, while weather replacement changes Hurricane accuracy. Electric and Rock pressure Ducklett but Lotad's Grass typing changes those matchups. Lotad resists allied Surf rather than being immune; its remaining HP and the current partner matter. Rocky Helmet and contact abilities punish Population Bomb. Against Ditto, scout its copied move and exploit the Choice lock rather than assuming it copies the opponent's remaining PP.

LOTAD @EVIOLITE SWIFT_SWIM TIMID 4/0/0/252/0/252 -1 | RAIN_DANCE, MUDDY_WATER, GIGA_DRAIN, ICE_BEAM | ivs=31/31/31/31/31/31 | friendship=255
DUCKLETT @EVIOLITE HYDRATION MODEST 252/0/4/252/0/0 -1 | HURRICANE, SURF, ICY_WIND, PROTECT | ivs=31/31/31/31/31/31 | friendship=255
DITTO @CHOICE_SCARF IMPOSTER RELAXED 252/0/252/0/4/0 0 | TRANSFORM | ivs=31/31/31/31/31/31 | friendship=255
TANDEMAUS @WIDE_LENS OWN_TEMPO JOLLY 4/252/0/0/0/252 0 | POPULATION_BOMB, SUPER_FANG, ENCORE, PROTECT | ivs=31/31/31/31/31/31 | friendship=255

# ---- E0007 Route104 cap=14 Stone Badge
## E0007 TRAINER_DARIAN class=regular
ai: Conservative
strategy: SETUP,PRESSURE
plan: Accept survivable chip for useful setup or attacking progress instead of preserving every HP with joint waiting. Chinchou's Discharge pressures both foes safely beside Ground-type Barboach, which can protect through enemy pressure or attack while remaining immune to its partner. Chinchou must account for friendly damage when Corphish or Clamperl replaces Barboach; targeted Thunder Wave and Muddy Water remain alternatives. Paralysis can make a later Dragon Dance speed crossing real, but neither setup nor paralysis is mandatory when direct attacks win now. Choice Band Adaptability Corphish supplies immediate physical power; Deep Sea Tooth Clamperl supplies immediate special power and uses Shell Smash only when its actual speed payoff and defense cost are worthwhile.
crack: Grass pressures all four species but must respect Ice Beam and neutral power. Electric absorption and Ground immunity can deny Chinchou's Discharge; removing Barboach removes its safe spread-attack partner. Electric types cannot be paralyzed by Thunder Wave. Oblivious protects Barboach from ordinary Taunt, but Clamperl can be Taunted and becomes frailer when it uses Shell Smash. Protect or redirection can exploit Corphish's Choice lock.

CHINCHOU @SITRUS_BERRY VOLT_ABSORB MODEST 252/0/4/252/0/0 -1 | THUNDER_WAVE, MUDDY_WATER, DISCHARGE, PROTECT | ivs=31/31/31/31/31/31 | friendship=255
BARBOACH @EVIOLITE OBLIVIOUS ADAMANT 252/252/4/0/0/0 -1 | DRAGON_DANCE, WATERFALL, HIGH_HORSEPOWER, PROTECT | ivs=31/31/31/31/31/31 | friendship=255
CORPHISH @CHOICE_BAND ADAPTABILITY ADAMANT 4/252/0/0/0/252 0 | CRABHAMMER, KNOCK_OFF, AQUA_JET, X_SCISSOR | ivs=31/31/31/31/31/31 | friendship=255
CLAMPERL @DEEP_SEA_TOOTH SHELL_ARMOR MODEST 252/0/4/252/0/0 0 | SHELL_SMASH, MUDDY_WATER, ICE_BEAM, PROTECT | ivs=31/31/31/31/31/31 | friendship=255

# ---- E0008 Route104 cap=14 Stone Badge
## E0008 TRAINER_CINDY_1 class=regular
ai: Conservative
strategy: NONE
plan: Snubbull's Intimidate and Milcery's Aroma Veil protect different parts of the opening. Either partner can use Helping Hand when the other's attack has the better payoff: physical Play Rough or Fire Punch, special Dazzling Gleam, or an incoming reserve's attack. Snubbull switches for defensive repositioning rather than repeatedly guarding. Milcery splits its special investment so Draining Kiss and Dazzling Gleam contribute when support is unnecessary, with Recover for meaningful survival windows. Fur Coat Furfrou invests in special defense and uses Snarl to soften slower special attackers, or U-turn to reopen a living Snubbull's Intimidate entry. Choice Band Slakoth retains Body Slam as its normal attack and adds Retaliate for the turn after an ally falls; the revenge window does not cancel Truant or justify a forced sacrifice.
crack: Steel and Poison threaten the Fairy pair, but respect Fire Punch coverage. Remove Aroma Veil before relying on Taunt or Heal Block. Milcery's offensive investment costs special bulk. Special attacks bypass Fur Coat but can be weakened by Snarl; Defiant or Competitive punish opposing stat drops. Protect can deny Slakoth's active turn and waste Retaliate's brief window; Ghosts exploit the lost Night Slash coverage. Critical hits ignore Attack drops, not Fur Coat itself.

SNUBBULL @LUM_BERRY INTIMIDATE ADAMANT 252/252/4/0/0/0 -1 | PLAY_ROUGH, FIRE_PUNCH, THUNDER_WAVE, HELPING_HAND | ivs=31/31/31/31/31/31 | friendship=255
MILCERY @EVIOLITE AROMA_VEIL BOLD 252/0/8/124/124/0 -1 | DRAINING_KISS, DAZZLING_GLEAM, HELPING_HAND, RECOVER | ivs=31/31/31/31/31/31 | friendship=255
FURFROU @SITRUS_BERRY FUR_COAT CAREFUL 252/0/4/0/252/0 0 | DOUBLE_EDGE, U_TURN, SUCKER_PUNCH, SNARL | ivs=31/31/31/31/31/31 | friendship=255
SLAKOTH @CHOICE_BAND TRUANT ADAMANT 252/252/4/0/0/0 0 | BODY_SLAM, HAMMER_ARM, RETALIATE, ROCK_SLIDE | ivs=31/31/31/31/31/31 | friendship=255

# ---- E0009 PetalburgWoods cap=14 Stone Badge
## E0009 TRAINER_LYLE class=casual
strategy: PRESSURE
plan: Accept nonlethal chip to make coordinated progress instead of preserving every HP with joint waiting; keeping a living teammate remains valuable. Nymble's Tinted Lens priority pressure opens beside Overcoat Sewaddle, which can attack physically or lower both foes' Special Attack with Struggle Bug. U-turn replaces Nymble's Protect so its fresh-entry priority role takes precedence over repeated waiting; direct switches remain available against Fake Out, and a pivot is useful only when its replacement is safe. First Impression belongs to a fresh entry, not every turn, and must not be forced through Fake Out or lethal focused attacks. Anorith supplies Rock Blast pressure on Flying foes and Aqua Jet finishing options. Specially invested Dottler supplies Psychic, correctly timed Reflect, and priority Helping Hand for a surviving physical partner. All four remain insects with different jobs.
crack: Fake Out can stop First Impression because it has higher priority; Protect can also waste that first-entry attack. Nymble cannot use Sucker Punch successfully into a status move. Rock and Flying pressure the leads but must respect Anorith's Rock attacks, and Dark types deny Dottler's Psychic while Taunt blocks Reflect and Helping Hand. Dottler is not a physical wall by item alone.

NYMBLE @FOCUS_SASH TINTED_LENS JOLLY 4/252/0/0/0/252 -2 | FIRST_IMPRESSION, LEECH_LIFE, SUCKER_PUNCH, U_TURN | ivs=31/31/31/31/31/31 | friendship=255

Stamina and Eviolite preserve Mudbray while Body Press converts accumulated Defense boosts into another attack alongside Ground and Rock coverage. One Icy Wind does not outrun every fast opponent, so choose attacks or protection according to the actual matchup. Morelull supplies Grass-resistant Spore support, conditional Strength Sap and spread Fairy damage. Eviolite Chinchou retains Water Absorb, special coverage and a slow Volt Switch option while adding physical durability to the reserve pair.
crack: Pressure Prinplup or punish Mudbray specially rather than feed Stamina with weak physical hits. Grass and powder immunity, Taunt and Poison pressure contest Morelull; Strength Sap fails to heal against relevant prevention and can interact badly with stat-drop-punishing abilities. Ground blocks Volt Switch, preventing its escape as well as its damage. Clear Body or Competitive changes the value of Icy Wind, and slow reserves cannot assume the same speed threshold as invested Mudbray.

plan: Shadow Tag Wobbuffet traps eligible foes, retaliates with Counter or Mirror Coat, and boosts its partner's attacks with Helping Hand. ## E0512 TRAINER_STEVEN class=rival
strategy: SETUP,TAILWIND
plan: Skarmory supports Rayquaza with Tailwind, while Cradily supplies Storm Drain and recovery, Melmetal threatens Policy-enhanced physical damage and Dialga supplies special coverage. Mega Metagross is the signature planned finish. Dragon Ascent also makes Rayquaza Mega-eligible; one shared Mega activation is allocated by the common AI, favoring Metagross on equivalent lines rather than forbidding a necessary winning Rayquaza transformation.
crack: Fire pressures Skarmory/Melmetal/Metagross but is neutral on Steel/Dragon Dialga. Ground/Fighting threatens the Steel core and Ice strongly pressures Rayquaza. Strong super-effective damage can KO Melmetal before Policy helps; inspect which Pokémon actually receives the Mega budget.

strategy: REDIRECTION
